        outputs

            item 1 Base.WAR_XM2010_BIPOD_DOWN,
        }

    {

        time = 30,

        category = Electrical,
        inputs

            item 1 tags[Flashlight;UsesBattery;TacLite] mode:keep flags[NotEmpty;ItemCount;InheritUsesAndEmpty],
        }
        outputs
        {
            item 1 Base.Battery,
        }
    }
